omnibox: Migrate OmniboxSuggestionButtonRowView buttons to ui::ColorId

Following crrev.com/c/8692528, `views::MdTextButton` supports
`ui::ColorVariant` for background and stroke overrides in addition to
text and vector-icon `ui::ColorId`s.

Pass `ui::ColorId`s directly to `OmniboxSuggestionRowChip` and
`OmniboxSuggestionRowButton` instead of resolving `SkColor`s in
`OnThemeChanged()` and `UpdateBackgroundColor()`, and remove the now
redundant overrides and unused chip theme state.

Screenshot: https://screenshot.googleplex.com/x5vdvsSZNKKtNJz
Bug: 394420459

298faf7ae871e831afb3e6c0e8bd061f4c806ea3 [ diff ] 
[webauthn] Route enclave discovery via CreatePlatformDiscoveries

Instead of plumbing an enclave EventStream through FidoDiscoveryFactory
and gating discovery creation on a separate consider_enclave flag in
FidoRequestHandlerBase::InitDiscoveries(), construct
EnclaveAuthenticatorDiscovery directly in
ChromeAuthenticatorRequestDelegate::ConfigureGpm() (unifying GPM
availability checks across regular and passkey upgrade requests) and
return it from CreatePlatformDiscoveries().

Attach additional_discoveries unconditionally in
FidoRequestHandlerBase::InitDiscoveries() so embedder-provided platform
discoveries (both EnclaveAuthenticatorDiscovery and the upcoming
GpmBrowserAuthenticatorDiscovery) still run on Windows when
MaybeCreateWinWebAuthnApiDiscovery() succeeds and InitDiscoveries()
erases kInternal from available_transports, as well as during passkey
upgrade requests that clear available_transports.

Removing consider_enclave is a no-op: FidoRequestHandlerBase's
constructor (used by GetAssertionRequestHandler) hardcoded it to true,
and MakeCredentialRequestHandler checked
request_.authenticator_attachment != kCrossPlatform even though
request_.authenticator_attachment is always kAny at construction time
(the RP's requested attachment is on options_.authenticator_attachment).
Excluding GPM when kCrossPlatform is requested (or when kInternal is
disallowed on GetAssertion) was already enforced in
AuthenticatorCommonImpl via discover_enclave, passed as
browser_provided_passkeys_available to ConfigureDiscoveries() to gate
ConfigureGpm().

Also fold FidoDiscoveryFactory::set_network_context_factory() into
set_cable_data(), its only remaining consumer.

Bug: 570170982

784358b92e2122944e84817d6d9e0ad032f75a79
parent
90d6d0c6eaacb0715283b3f65a9ff6b6beb1c317 [ diff ] 
[gpu] Separate read and write SyncTokens in ClientSharedImage

Previously, under UseAutomaticSyncTokenManagement, ClientSharedImage
stored all SyncTokens in a single `sync_token_map_`, causing read-only
accesses to unnecessarily wait on prior read-accesses.

This CL replaces `sync_token_map_` with a single `write_sync_token_` and
a per-sequence `read_sync_token_map_`:

- Read-only accesses wait only on `write_sync_token_` and store their
  completion token in `read_sync_token_map_`.

- Write accesses wait on both `write_sync_token_` and
  `read_sync_token_map_`, then update `write_sync_token_` and clear
  `read_sync_token_map_`.

- `ExportedSharedImage` (and its Mojom struct/traits) is updated to
  carry `write_sync_token` and `read_sync_tokens` separately instead of
  `managed_sync_tokens`.

In addition, this CL lets `SharedImageTexture`'s constructor wait
directly on `creation_sync_token()` instead of
`WaitSyncTokenInternal()`, since GL texture creation only depends on
SharedImage creation rather than subsequent writes.

Bug: 40286368, 575052822

8f571aa798f4a819fc9c02ca10c0ce6a622dbf7f [ diff ] 
ozone/evdev: exclude input event dispatch from safety checks

Add ExcludeEvdevDispatchFromSafetyChecks (disabled by default) to
guard base::ScopedSafetyChecksExclusion around OS input event
dispatch in ui::EventFactoryEvdev. On ChromeOS, kernel evdev input
events enter the browser UI thread as PostTask callbacks from
EventThreadEvdev rather than top-level message pump work items, so
PartitionAlloc scheduler-loop quarantine stays active across Ash and
Views input routing and regresses Ash.EventLatency.

parent
b4c13cd70c471bce86aca357966ce4d34486a778 [ diff ] 
[performance_manager] Add UMA metrics for restored tab loading outcomes

This CL adds SessionRestore.BackgroundTabLoading.TabCount{Outcome},
recording the number of tabs per outcome in a restore wave:
- (no suffix): Total scheduled tabs (sum of the outcomes below).
- .LoadedByPolicy: Load initiated by BackgroundTabLoadingPolicy.
- .AlreadyLoading: Already loading when scheduled (e.g. active tab).
- .LoadedExternally: Started loading while queued (e.g. user activated).
- .ClosedBeforeLoading: Closed before loading started.
- .SkippedForMaxTabs: Skipped at the 20-tab cap.
- .SkippedForFreeMemory: Skipped with < 150 MiB free physical memory.
- .SkippedForTabAge: Skipped because not visible for > 30 days.
- .StoppedForMemoryPressure: Dropped on moderate+ memory pressure.

A restore wave starts when ScheduleLoadForRestoredTabs() is called
while no restored tabs are pending, includes any overlapping calls, and
ends when all tracked restored tabs finish loading (or at shutdown).

These histograms will be used to assess the impact of planned changes
to BackgroundTabLoadingPolicy to reduce Chrome's total Private Memory
Footprint.

2f8dfaf61944858204db159e663e9c36ebb08148
parent
64a5dbcc73680d19e9a3a261d12ae663b8dd69c0 [ diff ] 
omnibox: Fill the context menu before the AI Mode page loads

The classic popup's "+" button opens a menu of tabs, inputs and tools
that acts on the AI Mode popup, and creates that popup's page if it
doesn't exist yet. The menu read those items from the page's
searchbox handler, which only exists once the page has loaded, so a
menu that created the page came up without them. Once the AI Mode
popup is created on demand, that happens on the first "+" click in
every window.

Read the input state model the handler will use, which exists before
the page loads. Move the tests that forced eager creation to work
around this back to their base fixtures, except on Mac, where the
classic popup then stays open after a tab is picked.

[iOS] Clean up `PolicyWatcherBrowserAgent` before refactoring

Non-functional cleanup ahead of replacing
`PolicyWatcherBrowserAgentObserver::OnSignInDisallowed()` with
`SceneSignInCommands`:
- Return early in
  `PolicyWatcherBrowserAgent::ForceSignOutIfSigninDisabled()` when
  sign-in is not disabled by policy.
- Remove dead `PolicyWatcherBrowserAgent::OnSignOutComplete()` (unused
  since https://crrev.com/c/6180164) along with its unused
  `profile_state.h` import and `//ios/chrome/app/profile` dependency.
- Remove unused `PolicyWatcherBrowserAgent` imports and dependency from
  `signin_policy_scene_agent.mm` and
  `ios/chrome/browser/policy/ui_bundled/BUILD.gn`, and directly import
  `browser.h`.

Test: ios_chrome_unittests --gtest_filter=PolicyWatcherBrowserAgent*

Sat Nov 21 15:03:11 2026
tree
f591b80f967ed319d0658a688fb70c8cd0fab399
parent
c20bf57fc509fbd3de376ad57880dcbeefc54404 [ diff ] 
Pass default SecurityOptions for worker URLLoaderFactory in WebView

Worker requests previously passed std::nullopt for security_options, which left reject_cors_request_ disabled in AndroidStreamReaderURLLoader. Consequently, CORS-mode requests redirected to content:// or asset URLs could open the stream under the host app UID before the response was dropped by CORS checks.

This change passes default-initialized SecurityOptions (accounting for kDisableWebSecurity) to worker loader factories, matching frame factory behavior and ensuring CORS requests to these schemes are rejected before attempting to open the stream.

Bug: 533068965

[variations] Support hybrid PQ seed signatures and key rotation

Pin three hybrid ECDSA P-256 + ML-DSA-44 Variations seed signing
keys (Key IDs 1, 2, and 3) matching Tink's
COMPOSITE_SIGN_MLDSA_44_ECDSA_P256_TINK_RAW profile, and wire the
client-advertised key protocol across C++, Android, WebView, and
iOS first-run fetchers.

- Send X-Seed-Key-Ids (1,2,3,0 while legacy is still trusted;
  1,2,3 once a new-key seed is stored) on seed fetch requests.
- Reuse X-Seed-Signature with a <key_id>:<base64_sig> prefix for
  hybrid signatures, keeping legacy Base64 signatures supported
  until a valid new-key seed is verified and stored.
- Verify hybrid ML-DSA-44 + ECDSA P-256 signatures via BoringSSL
  and persist kVariationsLegacySeedKeyDistrusted in Local State
  once a valid new-key seed is stored (flushing both the SeedFile
  and Local State on transition, and healing on load). On Chrome,
  subsequent legacy-only seeds are rejected while preserving the
  previous valid seed.
- Fix kIdenticalToSafeSeedSentinel aliasing to require matching
  signatures as well as seed bytes.
- WebView persists only kVariationsLegacySeedKeyDistrusted per
  embedding app and re-imports the seed from the WebView service
  on every startup. VariationsSeedFetcher ignores legacy-only
  responses when the stored seed already has a hybrid prefix; if
  an app that distrusts legacy is still handed a legacy-only seed
  (e.g. after a WebView provider switch), it rejects the seed and
  runs with no variations until a hybrid seed is fetched.

[LiveCaption] Re-enable Live Caption browsertests on Linux

This CL re-enables `LiveCaptionControllerTest.OnSodaError` and
`LiveCaptionSpeechRecognitionHostTest.DestroysWithoutCrashing`.

`LiveCaptionControllerTest.OnSodaError` was disabled in
https://crrev.com/c/4951015 (https://crbug.com/40936746) due to test
expectation failures following the introduction of multi-language Live
Caption. Originally, SODA installation errors toggled
`prefs::kLiveCaptionEnabled` off. With multi-language support finalized
in https://crrev.com/c/7037377, the controller no longer turns off the
preference upon error, delegating user-facing error reporting to
`CaptionsHandler` in the Settings WebUI. This CL updates the assertions
to verify that `prefs::kLiveCaptionEnabled` remains enabled when SODA
install errors occur.

`LiveCaptionSpeechRecognitionHostTest.DestroysWithoutCrashing` was
disabled in https://crrev.com/c/2940284 (https://crbug.com/40184759) due
to flakiness across platforms. The test navigated to external
`http://www.google.com`, which triggered unmocked network safety aborts
in browser test environments. This CL switches navigation to use
`embedded_test_server()->GetURL("/title1.html")` and asserts that
`new_frame_host` differs from `frame_host`.

Tested on Linux.

Part of http://go/arthurmilchior-fix-all-test-skill-chrome for AI week.

Fixed: 40936746
Fixed: 40184759

Ash: Migrate AmbientControllerTest off RunUntilIdle

This changelist is part of an ongoing series to modernize Ash tests by
migrating them away from RunUntilIdle() and toward targeted
synchronization with APIs such as RunUntil(), TestFuture, RunLoop
callbacks, observer waiters, or exact mock-time advancement.

HideAmbientScreen(), UnlockScreen() and SetExternalUsbPowerConnected()
(USB power on a discharging battery does not count as a charger) reach
ReleaseWakeLock() synchronously, but CancelWakeLock() travels over
mojo, so the TestWakeLockProvider count drops in a posted task. These
five sites now call WaitForDisplayWakeLockRelease(), which waits until
the provider reports that no kPreventDisplaySleep wake lock is held.
The calls after SetExternalPowerConnected() and SetPowerStateCharging()
are deleted: both call OnPowerStatusChanged() directly, which returns
without touching the wake lock because a charger is connected, so
nothing is posted before HideAmbientScreen().

Bug: b:506794349
Test: CQ

Ash: Migrate AmbientControllerDurationTest off RunUntilIdle

This changelist is part of an ongoing series to modernize Ash tests by
migrating them away from RunUntilIdle() and toward targeted
synchronization with APIs such as RunUntil(), TestFuture, RunLoop
callbacks, observer waiters, or exact mock-time advancement.

HideAmbientScreen() and UnlockScreen() reach ReleaseWakeLock()
synchronously, but CancelWakeLock() travels over mojo, so the
TestWakeLockProvider count drops in a posted task. Both sites now call
a new AmbientAshTestBase helper, WaitForDisplayWakeLockRelease(), which
registers a WakeLockObserver through NotifyOnWakeLockDeactivation()
and waits until the provider reports that no kPreventDisplaySleep wake
lock is held. RunUntil() won't do here: under MOCK_TIME it first
fast-forwards through pending timers, such as the inactivity timer
HideAmbientScreen() starts, which shows ambient again and re-acquires
the lock.

Bug: b:506794349
Test: CQ

Ash: Migrate AutoclickTest off RunUntilIdle

This changelist is part of an ongoing series to modernize Ash tests by
migrating them away from RunUntilIdle() and toward targeted
synchronization with APIs such as RunUntil(), TestFuture, RunLoop
callbacks, observer waiters, or exact mock-time advancement.

ConfirmationDialogShownWhenDisablingFeature now waits with
WidgetDestroyedWaiter for the dialog widget's asynchronous close, which
clears GetDisableDialogForTesting(); the dialog callbacks already set
the enabled state synchronously. AutoclickScrollEvents and
ScrollMenuBubblePostioning now set a nonzero autoclick delay and
fast-forward mock time by it, so each dwell has set the scroll location
before the next call that uses it. MovementThreshold's drain is dropped
because UpdateDisplay() creates the second root window synchronously.
The calls in SetUp() and WaitForMouseEvents() stay: SetUp() settles the
tasks that display setup leaves, and the helper waits out a zero-delay
dwell for 45 callers, many of which expect no events. Under this
fixture's mock time, RunUntil() fast-forwards through every pending
delayed task before it checks and never returns.

216e7552d09802235eae63f102b9b10c9d62c1d4 [ diff ] 
Ash: Migrate AmbientDlcBackgroundInstallerTest off RunUntilIdle

This changelist is part of an ongoing series to modernize Ash tests by
migrating them away from RunUntilIdle() and toward targeted
synchronization with APIs such as RunUntil(), TestFuture, RunLoop
callbacks, observer waiters, or exact mock-time advancement.

The three tests that expect an install now use RunUntil() to wait until
the Time of Day DLC install request reaches the fake dlcservice client.
That request is made on the main thread, in the reply to the installer's
thread pool task, so a task on this thread makes the wait true. A
test-only subclass of FakeDlcserviceClient counts the requests, because
the fake reports installed DLCs only through a posted callback. The
RunUntilIdle() call in InstallsOnlyOnce after setting the service idle
is deleted: the network test helper's SetServiceProperty() already runs
the main thread until idle, so NetworkStateHandler sees the service go
idle before it is set online again. The remaining RunUntilIdle() calls
in InstallsOnlyOnce and DoesNotInstallWhenFeatureDisabled stay: each
precedes a check that no further install happened, and when the
installer correctly does nothing, nothing observable marks the point by
which a wrongly started install would have reached the fake.

Test: CQ

https://crrev.com/c/7896031 added
//chrome/browser/ui/webui/print_preview:interactive_ui_tests to the
interactive_ui_tests deps without the enable_print_preview guard. The
print_preview BUILD.gn asserts enable_print_preview, so the build breaks
when enable_print_preview is false. Move the dep into an
enable_print_preview block.

6be7988affc55f751c146961001b5f1f6d90de62 [ diff ] 
android: Fix DevTools closing immediately for Incognito tabs

When DevTools is opened for an Incognito tab, the DevTools frontend
WebContents belongs to the primary OTR profile. DevToolsActivity always
attached it to the regular TabModel, so the frontend was never inserted,
and the activity closed itself right after removing its initial blank
tab because it had no tabs left.

Make DevToolsActivity aware of the frontend's profile:
- Pass whether the frontend belongs to the primary OTR profile to the
  activity, and report CustomTabProfileType.INCOGNITO from
  DevToolsIntentDataProvider in that case.
- Attach the frontend to the TabModel matching its profile.
- Share the primary OTR profile instead of using the unique OTR profile
  that Incognito CCTs get by default, and register the activity as an
  IncognitoTabHost so that the primary OTR profile is not destroyed
  while DevTools still uses it (e.g. right after the last Incognito tab
  is closed).

To do so, add BrowserServicesIntentDataProvider#usesPrimaryOtrProfile(),
which consolidates the existing "Incognito popup opened by Chrome"
condition used by BaseCustomTabActivity (profile selection) and
CustomTabIncognitoManager (IncognitoTabHost registration), and override
it in DevToolsIntentDataProvider.

Also:
- Disallow DevTools for pages in non-primary OTR profiles (e.g.
  Incognito or Ephemeral Custom Tabs), which are destroyed with their
  hosting activity, in DevToolsWindow::AllowDevToolsFor() on Android.
- Abort recreated or missing-WebContents DevToolsActivity launches in
  maybeDispatchLaunchIntent() before cookie restoration or tab creation
  runs, and finish if existingTab is null during
  finishNativeInitialization().

Closing the inspected Incognito tab still closes DevTools, same as for
regular tabs.

Automation: http://go/massan-jt/0b4346d9-4435-4664-a06a-92a488ccd26f

[SRI Message Signatures] Update tests and drop legacy "sri" tag

`ParseSRIMessageSignaturesFromHeaders` accepted the legacy `tag="sri"`
parameter while tests and origin trial participants migrated to
`tag="ed25519-integrity"`.

This CL updates the remaining unit tests, WPTs, WebBundle test
resources, and inspector-protocol tests to use `tag="ed25519-integrity"`
(which mostly consisted of recomputing the affected Ed25519 signatures);
removes the `"sri"` tag fallback from `sri_message_signatures.cc`; and
updates `SRIMessageSignatureParserTest` to verify that `"sri"` is
ignored like other non-SRI tags.

996198757d191b8e1680434fb28061c576de3d62

cb6ff50db2898f2e670709bc8892e2bdc0c7d8c8 [ diff ] 
[CSP] Check all expression fields in CSPSourceListIsNone and IsSelf

`CSPSourceListIsNone` and `CSPSourceListIsSelf` duplicated a check
across twelve fields of `CSPSourceList` and omitted four newer fields:
`allow_inline_speculation_rules`, `allow_dynamic_url`, `url_hashes`,
and `eval_hashes`. As a result, a directive containing only those
expressions (such as `script-src 'inline-speculation-rules'`) was
misclassified as `'none'` or `'self'`, including in the reasonable
restriction metrics.

This CL centralizes the check, and updates the list to include the
additional fields.

5704ca9c5980e15b7fe5702e178799ecd0cfbf33
parent
da15ed75d0263bef51185a44bd3438c9dd3277f9 [ diff ] 
network: Add ResourceRequest::content_user_agent

To let scripts set the User-Agent request header, the browser's own
User-Agent (including DevTools and embedder overrides) has to travel
separately from a script-supplied one: the first must stay CORS-exempt,
while the second must trigger a CORS preflight and must not be copied
onto that preflight. Keeping both in the request headers cannot express
this, and a renderer-supplied flag cannot be trusted to exempt one of
them.

This adds an optional content_user_agent field to ResourceRequest. The
network service sends it as the User-Agent only when the request has no
explicit User-Agent, and CORS preflights use it instead of the request's
User-Agent header when the new AllowUserAgentHeaderFromScript feature is
enabled. CorsURLLoaderFactory rejects the field while the feature is
disabled and truncates it to 512 bytes otherwise; a histogram records
its size.

With the feature disabled, which is the default, this CL changes no
behavior; nothing sets the field yet. This is the first of a series of
CLs; later ones fill the field from Blink and handle redirects, service
workers, extensions and WebView.

Enabling the feature with only this CL changes one thing: CORS
preflights stop copying the User-Agent from the request headers, so a
DevTools or embedder override that Blink still puts there is not sent on
the preflight. A later CL moves that override to content_user_agent.

The feature is declared in //net because a later CL also checks it in
net::HttpUtil::IsSafeHeader(), which decides whether User-Agent is a
forbidden header, and //net cannot depend on //services/network.

Bug: 40450316

Replace legacy eula_text.html URLs with /chrome/terms/

The legacy 2014 ChromeOS EULA page (eula_text.html) is obsolete and
being removed in favor of the updated Chrome/ChromeOS Additional Terms
of Service at https://www.google.com/chrome/terms/ (matching
IDS_CHROME_ADDITIONAL_TERMS_OF_SERVICE_URL and
ash::external_urls::kCrosEulaOnlineURLPath).

- Update IDS_TOS_NOTIFICATION_LINK in chrome/app/generated_resources.grd
  from https://www.google.com/chrome/privacy/eula_text.html to
  https://www.google.com/chrome/terms/.
- Add unit tests for AnnouncementNotificationService::GetAnnouncementURL
  covering both the default fallback URL and the kAnnouncementUrl param.
- Update FakeEulaMixin to serve /intl/en-US/chrome/terms/ instead of
  /intl/en-US/chrome/eula_text.html.

Visual verification (ChromeOS Ash with local test server serving the
corresponding static HTML pages):
- Screencast (Before vs After): https://screencast.googleplex.com/cast/NTYxOTY0Njc5NDMwMTQ0MHxlNzU1MGU5Ni1mMA
- Before: https://screenshot.googleplex.com/UsL5Xo8zSukoQse
- After: https://screenshot.googleplex.com/e5c8YSMm9SfAbyx
- After (OOBE FakeEulaMixin): https://screenshot.googleplex.com/NGo6oWKPWqXyfos

Test: unit_tests --gtest_filter="*AnnouncementNotificationService*"
Test: browser_tests --gtest_filter="GuestTosScreenTest.*"
Test: browser_tests --gtest_filter="*ConsolidatedConsentScreenTest.*"
Test: browser_tests --gtest_filter="LoginAfterUpdateToFlexTest.*"

7610908a5d2d7b2587f8836a0f42cf2c57d94944
parent
522060fe049a1b64a463dee2bafe51ec5d2aa6d6 [ diff ] 
content: Cancel the date/time picker when its WebContents moves

The date/time picker dialog belongs to the window it was opened in. If
the WebContents moves to a new Activity while the picker is open, e.g. a
WebView WebContent across a rotation or a reparented Chrome tab, the
dialog stays behind and never reports back. Blink keeps waiting for it,
so no date or time input on the page can open a picker again, and the
old Activity leaks until the page navigates or closes.

Like SelectPopup and TextSuggestionPopupController, DateTimeChooserAndroid
now cancels the request when the WebContents leaves its window, and
native's CancelDialog() dismisses the dialog and releases the Java object
once the dialog reports back, rather than holding on to it and the
Activity it references until the next picker opens.

Bug: 531633054
Test: out/Default/bin/run_content_junit_tests -f '*DateTimeChooserAndroidTest*'
Test: out/Default/bin/run_content_browsertests --gtest_filter='DateTimeChooserBrowserTest.*'

4593cbd6d29e0ce265d488e0e22c41fb242a2a39 [ diff ] 
[contextual-tasks] Move system voice search check out of mixin

Only Contextual Tasks sets `isSystemVoiceSearchEnabled`, so handling it
in `ComposeboxEmbedderMixin.onVoiceSearchButtonClick()` forced shared
cr_components tests to stub it. Move the check to an override in the
Contextual Tasks inner composebox and remove those unused stubs.

Add internal Blink web tests for `navigator.playHaptics()`

Haptic output is invisible to web pages, so standard web tests cannot
check whether calls reach the browser. Intercepting `HapticsService`
with MojoJS lets Blink verify renderer dispatch, gating, clamping, and
input validation without hardware.

Bug: 531787872

dbe47738479e9eb1990992f6079cb646b7698d87
parent
5c7518a7b06bc2610bb8c711af66967d9fef8f13 [ diff ] 
WebNN: Enable OpenVINO NPU logging with --webnn-ort-logging-level

Debugging the OpenVINO NPU plugin requires setting its own LOG_LEVEL in
the OpenVINO EP's load_config. Today that means editing the source,
since the load_config session config entry is fixed by WebNN.

Reuse --webnn-ort-logging-level instead. When the logging level is INFO
or VERBOSE, ApplyEpConfigEntries sets NPU.LOG_LEVEL to LOG_INFO in the
OpenVINO EP's load_config at runtime, so the JSON in config_entries
stays the single source of truth.

LOG_INFO gives the NPU compiler version, target device ID, compile time,
peak memory, blob size and batching decisions. LOG_DEBUG and LOG_TRACE
add per-pass and per-inference tracing, which is too noisy to be useful
here. LOG_LEVEL is only set for NPU because the GPU plugin does not
support it and rejects unknown options. It only affects logging, not the
compiled blob, so it is safe to apply in both the Compiler process and
the GPU process that imports the compiled model.

Ideally the EP would derive LOG_LEVEL from the session logging severity;
a TODO tracks removing this once ORT supports that.

Also add IsOrtInfoLoggingEnabled() and use it wherever the INFO or
VERBOSE level is checked, so these checks stay consistent.

Mark Storage#key() and Storage#getItem() with [Affects=Nothing]

Allow DevTools side-effect-free evaluation (throwOnSideEffect: true) on
Storage#key() and Storage#getItem() (matching the default-safe named
interceptor on Storage) instead of aborting with EvalError, per Blink's
[Affects=Nothing] contract:
https://source.chromium.org/chromium/chromium/src/+/main:third_party/blink/renderer/bindings/IDLExtendedAttributes.md;l=566-580

0cfae948c88a94f60a0ae76e2a67f51b875b5971 [ diff ] 
[VerticalTabs] Initialize root view before SideUi registration

Initialize VerticalTabsSideUiCoordinator#mRootView before registering
with SideUiCoordinator.
-Previously, registerSideUiContainer(this) was called before mRootView
 was created, so getView() would return null during registration.
-Now, mRootView and its layout params are initialized before
 registering with SideUiCoordinator.

request::GetJSONContentMatcher() and json:: matchers, so nothing calls
GetContentMatcher(). Remove it and its tests.
